Document 004: 10月企業物価指数 ～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～

Economic Indicators

2026 年 11 月 12 日

1/2 
10 月企業物価指数 
～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  阿原 健一郎 
（℡：050-5470-4868）

1. 国内企業物価指数は幾分加速 
 本日公表された 10 月の国内企業物価指数は前年比＋7.1％と、前月（同＋6.8％）から 
伸びが幾分加速した。前年比寄与度で内訳を見ると、玄米や豚肉などの「農林水産物」のマイナ
ス寄与が前月から拡大した一方、 「電力・都市ガス・水道」や「石油・石炭製品」 、 「化学製品」の
プラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表１） 。 
 背景には、原油やナフサの価格上昇が石油製品や化学製品へ波及していることに加え、電力に
おいても、過去の輸入燃料価格の上昇がタイムラグを伴って燃料費調整単価に反映され、全体を
押し上げていることがある。農林水産物の価格下落が一部で下押し要因となっているものの、エ
ネルギー関連を中心としたコスト上昇圧力は依然として根強い。

２. 輸入物価指数はわずかに加速 
 10 月の輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.8％と、前月（同＋23.8％）から伸びがわずかに加速した。
「石油・石炭・天然ガス」のプラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表
２） 。 
 為替変動の影響を除いた契約通貨ベースでも同＋19.2％と、前月（同＋17.3％）から伸びが拡大
しており、海外の資源価格上昇が輸入物価を押し上げている。輸入コストの増加が時間差を伴っ
て国内の製品価格に波及することを踏まえると、先行きも価格転嫁を通じて消費者物価への上昇
圧力が残る可能性がある。

以 上

欧州大規模デモが問う財政再建の道 
金融市場が動揺するリスクは当面残存する見通し

○ このところ欧州ではインフレに伴う生活苦や公共サービスの劣化等を背景に各地で大規模抗議活
動が発生している 
○ 大規模デモは緊縮を伴う財政健全化を難しくする要因となる。財政懸念や政情不安は国債利回り
の上昇やユーロ安を招いており、経済への悪影響も懸念される 
○ 来年にかけて、欧州では複数の重要な国政選挙が予定される中、政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金
融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存するだろう

１．欧州で広がる大規模抗議活動 
このところ、欧州では大規模デモが多発している（図表1）。フランスでは、9月以降、教員不足や
学校施設の不備など、教育環境の改善を求める高校生の抗議活動が続いていた。こうした中で、10月
半ばには、物価高への対策や公共サービスの改善を求める市民も加わり、抗議活動は全国規模に拡大
した。 
大規模デモが発生しているのはフランスだけではない。フランスのデモに一部触発される形でベル

ギーやイタリア等でも抗議活動が発生している。また、スペインでは、高齢女性が家賃を支払えずに
強制退去させられたことをきっかけに、住宅不足や住宅価格・家賃高騰等に対する不満が爆発する形
で大規模な抗議活動が発生し、これが遠因となりサンチェス首相は議会の解散に追い込まれた。 
各地で発生する大規模抗議活動の背景には、近年のインフレによる生活苦やこれを受けた政府への
不満がある1。EUにおいて、生活苦を訴える人の割合は低中所得層を中心に2022年以降高まっている
ほか、 自国が直面する課題としてインフレや経済状況を挙げる人の割合も高い （図表2）。これらの要
因が、自国政府に対する信頼感の低迷につながっている（図表3）。 
本稿では、生活改善を求めて各国で広がる抗議活動が経済や財政、金融市場に与える影響について
考察する。 
２．大規模抗議活動は財政健全化を阻む要因に 
一連の抗議活動は、2018年にフランスで発生した黄色いベスト運動を想起させる2。 当時の参加者は、
自動車に常備が義務付けられている黄色い安全ベストを着用し、燃料税の増税や生活費高騰に抗議し
た。購買力の低下や政府への不満が背景にある点は当時と共通している。異なるのは、政府の追加的
な財政支出の余地が、当時と比べてさらに狭まっていることである。 
欧州債務危機が起きた2010年代前半以降、順調に進捗してきた政府債務の削減は、2020年代に入る
と足踏みしはじめた。パンデミック時の大規模給付やウクライナ戦争後のエネルギー高対策など、危
機対応の財政出動が頻発したためだ。黄色いベスト運動が発生した2018年と比べて政府債務が増加し
たEU加盟国は、27カ国中18カ国にのぼる（図表4）。 
加えて、足元では防衛費増額や社会保障費・利払い負担の拡大など、構造的な財政支出圧力も高ま
っている。 各国の次年度予算案を踏まえると、 財政赤字は当面、高止まりする公算が大きい （図表5） 。
安全保障環境の変化やエネルギー価格の高騰を踏まえ、EUは一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連の追加支
出をEU財政ルールの対象外とする柔軟化措置を設けている。 しかし、 当該措置を適用しているドイツ

3

やベルギーでは、特例対象の追加支出を除いてもなお、財政赤字をGDP比3％に抑制するEUの基準を
超過する見込みである。それでもベルギーでは、連立与党間の予算協議が決裂し、首相が辞意を表明
する事態に陥った。 
フランスを起点に広がった抗議活動は、 緊縮を伴う財政健全化の難しさを浮き彫りにしている。2018
年の黄色いベスト運動では、マクロン政権が燃料税増税の撤回や所得支援策の拡充に踏み切ったこと
で、抗議活動が漸く沈静化に向かった。今回は、11月に新たなデモやストライキが計画されるなど、
いまだ抗議活動が収束する見通しは立っていない。各国政府は事態の長期化を回避するため、家計支
援や公共サービス拡充などの対応を迫られる可能性があり、財政拡張圧力が一段と強まることが懸念
される。 
３．政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存 
金融市場は、欧州主要国の財政状況に対する警戒を強めている。 
フランスをはじめとする高債務国では、政治や財政を巡る混乱を嫌気して、10年債利回りが上昇し
ている（図表6）。2010年代の欧州債務危機を受けてECB（欧州中央銀行）は、条件付きで対象国の国
債を無制限に購入可能なOMT（Outright Monetary Transactions）や経済の基礎的条件では正当化されな
い国債市場の混乱が金融政策の波及を阻害する場合に発動可能なTPI （Transmission Protection 
Instrument）などの市場安定化措置3を整備してきた。当時よりセーフティーネットが整備されている
こともあり、足元の国債利回り上昇が欧州債務危機時のような金融危機につながる可能性は現時点で
は低い。 
とはいえ、金利の上昇は景気下押しや、さらなる財政悪化の要因となる。金利上昇幅が大きいフラ
ンスを例に足元の利回り上昇幅を踏まえて試算すると、設備投資・住宅投資が1年後に3％程度減少す
るとの結果が得られる （図表7） 。金利上昇やそれに伴う景気減速は、利払い費の増加や税収の減少を
図表5 主要国の財政収支実績と見通し 図表6 ユーロ圏の高債務国と 
ドイツの10年債利回り

きなかった国。数値は成立前の政府予算案ベース。

通じて、財政をさらに悪化させる要因にもなる。 
各国の政情不安や財政への懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営をも左右する可能性がある。足元では、ユ
ーロが主要通貨に対して下落している （図表8） 。 ユーロ安は、 輸入物価の上昇を通じてインフレ率を
押し上げる要因となる。足元のユーロ下落幅を前提に、時系列モデルを用いてユーロ圏のインフレ率
への影響を試算すると、総合インフレ率は0.3％Pt程度、コアインフレ率は0.1％Pt程度押し上げられる
との結果が得られた（図表9）。 ユーロ圏では、イラン情勢の緊迫化に伴うエネルギー価格の高騰を背
景に、直近でもインフレ率が加速している。先行きユーロ安圧力が強い状況が続けば、インフレ率に
一段の上昇圧力がかかる可能性がある。しかし、ECBはインフレ圧力が一段と強まっても、状況によ
っては追加利上げに踏み切りにくくなることが想定される。利上げがさらなる長期金利の上昇、国債
市場の不安定化を招くおそれがあるためだ。このように政情不安や財政懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営
を困難にする要因にもなりかねない。 
各国の財政や政治情勢に対する金融市場の懸念は当面残存する可能性が高い。欧州では、今年11月
にスペイン議会選挙、来年にはフランス大統領選挙やイタリア議会選挙など、重要な国政選挙が相次
いで予定されている。大規模デモの発生に加えて選挙も控える中、各国政府は国民に負担を求める緊
縮的な財政政策を例年以上に打ち出しにくくなっている。また、各国の選挙では極右政党やポピュリ
スト政党の勢力拡大が予想され、選挙後の政治はより一層不安定化するリスクがある。このほか、コ
ロナ禍からの経済復興を目的に設立されたEU復興基金の受け取りが終了することも、 来年以降の財政
懸念を強める要因になるだろう。同基金の配分が手厚かった南欧や中東欧諸国は、自国の財政負担を
抑えながら公共投資などを通じて経済成長を支えてきたが、来年以降はこうした効果が徐々に剥落す
ることになる。

以上のように、2020年代に入ってから、コロナ禍におけるサプライチェーン混乱、ロシアのウクラ
イナ侵攻やイラン情勢の緊迫によるエネルギー高が立て続けに起きてインフレが継続する中、欧州各
国で有権者の生活コスト上昇に対する不満が強まっている。一方、これらの緊急事態に対応するため
財政出動を繰り返してきたことに加え、高齢化に伴う社会保障費の増加、地政学リスクの高まりを背
景とした防衛費増強の必要性、利払い費の増大に直面しており、各国の財政余力は著しく低下してい
る。来年にかけて予定されている主要国の選挙では、極右・ポピュリスト政党の勢力拡大が予想され
ており、政治の混乱に拍車がかかる懸念もある。欧州の政治情勢や財政状況に対する不安が来年にか
けて沈静化するとは考えにくく、国債市場や為替市場が動揺するリスクに引き続き留意する必要があ
ろう。

1 先進 18 カ国のパネルデータを用いた分析を行っている Federle et al.（2024）は、インフレ率が事前の予想を 1％Pt 上回り、な
おかつ実質賃金の伸び率が当該国の長期平均を下回る場合、反政府デモの発生件数が約 7.9％増加すると推計している 
2 黄色いベスト運動の主催者らは、10 月以降、フランス各地で行われているデモに加わるとともに、さらなる抗議行動への参加
を呼びかけている 
3 OMT や TPI は、発動要件やその必要性等の点から慎重な判断が求められることもあり、これまでに一度も発動された事例は
ない。こうした中、一部では 2024 年末に終了した PEPP（Pandemic Emergency Purchase Program）の再投資を再開し、足元の金
利上昇に対応する可能性を指摘する声もある

Document 066: 2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

US Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2026 年 10 月 FOMC プレビュー 
～インフレ認識と AI 投資の持続性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 FOMC（10/27～28 開催）では政策金利が 3.75～4.00％に据え置かれる可能性が高い。
FRB は 9 月に約 3 年振りとなる利上げに踏み切った一方、追加の政策調整を急がず、今後の
経済指標を見極める姿勢を示すとみられる。 
○ ウォーシュ議長の記者会見では、予想を上振れるインフレ動向に関する評価、及び一部のAI
クラウド業者による信用不安が今後の設備投資に与える影響を巡って、何らかの見解が示さ
れるかが注目される。もっとも、市場で織り込まれる12 月 FOMCの利上げを巡っては、具体
的な言及を避ける可能性が高い。 
○ FRBは5つのタスクフォースのうちの生産性と雇用（Productivity and Jobs）部会におい
て、11月16 日にサンフランシスコで公聴会を開催する予定だ。年末までに取りまとめられ
る提言や議論の進捗に関して、ウォーシュ議長から何らかの言及があるかが注目される。

### Page 2

2/5 
・据え置きが見込まれる 10 月FOMC 
2026 年10月FOMC（10/27～28開催）において、FRBは賛成多数で政策金利（3.75～4.00%）を据
え置く可能性が高い。FRBは 9 月FOMCにて米国経済の堅調な推移やインフレ高止まりを踏まえ、23
年 7 月以来となる0.25%の利上げに踏み切った。また、同時に公表されたドットチャートで年内に
追加で 1回の利上げ見通しが示された。8 月CPI及びFRBのタカ派的なスタンスを踏まえて、金融市
場は連続利上げの可能性を徐々に織り込んでいったものの、FRB高官は9月末から10 月初めにか
けてこれに慎重なスタンスを示した。例えば、9/29にウィリアムズ総裁は「急ぐ必要はなく、情
報を集める時間がある」 、10/1 にジェファーソン副議長は「 （政策判断には）もう少し時間がかか
るかもしれない」とそれぞれ述べた。 
この結果、本稿執筆時点におけるFF金利先物市場（Fed Watch）において、83.9%の確率で金利
据え置きが見込まれる一方、0.25%ptの利上げ予想は16.1%に留まる（資料1）。なお、金融市場は
12 月FOMCにおける0.25%の利上げに加えて、2027年末に向けて更に 2回程度の利上げを織り込ん
でいる（資料 2）。この場合の合計利上げ幅は+1.0%ptとなり、9月時点のドットチャート（26年に
計 0.5%ptの利上げを行い、27 年は据え置き）を上回る。もっとも、過去の利上げ局面ではそれが
1～2 回に留まることは稀であり、金融市場はこうした過去の事例も一部に織り込んでいるとみら
れる（詳細は 2026年 9月 18日付け「ＦＲＢの過去の利上げサイクルと長期金利の行方」）。

資料 1：10月 FOMCにおける利上げ織り込み 
  資料 2：市場が予想する各FOMCにおける政策金利 の確率分布（10/21時点）

出所: CME、FRB より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成

地区で小幅から緩やかに拡大した（2 地区がmoderate growth、3地区がmodest growth、4 地区が

### Page 3

3/5 
slight growth） 」とまとめられた。また、 「労働市場は総じて変化がなかった」と指摘した。一
方、「物価上昇は3 地区で力強く上昇し、8 地区で緩やかに上昇した。8地区のうち3地区では投
入価格の上昇圧力が強まっている」とインフレ高止まりへの懸念が強調された。また、複数の地
区でインフレによる生活費負担の増大が賃金の上昇圧力になっている可能性が指摘された。 
10 月FOMCまでには10/27に 9 月耐久財受注や10月コンファレンスボード消費者信頼感指数など
が公表されるものの、10月FOMCの政策判断に大きな影響を与える可能性は低い。

・インフレ認識、AI投資の持続性、タスクフォース 
9 月FOMCの声明文では「経済活動は堅調に拡大している」との景気認識が維持された。一方、 「イ
ンフレ率は高止まりしている（7 月時点：インフレ率は2%目標に対して高止まりしている。これは
エネルギーを含む一部セクターにおける供給ショックを通じた価格上昇を反映している） 」と、供
給ショックに関する言及が前回から削除された。10 月FOMCにおいても、こうした景気・物価認識は
概ね維持される可能性が高い。一方、物価認識に関して、足下のインフレ減速の鈍化や加速の兆し
を指摘する、或いはインフレ高止まりの背景として需要の強さに言及する場合、タカ派的なスタン
スと捉えられるだろう。 
ウォーシュ議長は記者会見において、次回 12 月FOMCに関する具体的な言及を避けるとみられる
一方、下記3 点に関して新たな見解が示されるかが焦点となろう。 
まず、足下のインフレ認識に変化があるかだ。9 月FOMCの記者会見で議長は「インフレ率は高止
まりしており、その状態はあまりに長く続いている」や「あまりに多くの品目が 3％を超えるイン
フレ率にある」と述べた。また、同会合の議事要旨では何名か（some）の参加者が「AI普及を通じ
て、需要が中期的に供給を上回り、インフレ上昇への圧力となる可能性がある」と指摘した。その
後公表された 9 月のコアCPIは予想を上振れるなど、高関税による財価格の押し上げやエネルギー
高による輸送サービスへの波及の兆しがみられる。ウォーシュ議長がこうした需要と供給双方のイ
ンフレ要因に関して、新たな見解を示すのかが注目される。 
次に、AI投資の持続性への見方だ。FOMC後の10/29に公表される7～9月期実質GDP成長率は前期
から加速すると見込まれ、AI関連を中心とした設備投資の拡大、及び資産効果（株高）による消費
の堅調さが示されるとみられる。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16 の講演において「世界経済は 5～6 か月
前に予測できなかったほどの回復力を示している」と述べ、エネルギー高や利上げに対する経済的
な耐性を強調した。一方、足下ではAIモデル開発業者などに計算資源を提供する「ネオクラウド企
業」の一部で信用不安が浮上しており、データセンターを中心としたAI関連投資が減速するとの懸
念が強まっている。ウォーシュ議長がAI投資の先行き、及び一部企業の信用不安が金融市場全体に
与える影響に関して、どのような見解を示すのかが焦点となる。 
最後に、タスクフォースの進捗状況が部分的にでも示され、2027年以降の政策運営に関するヒン
トがみられるかだ。ウォーシュ議長は就任後の6月に 5 つのタスクフォース（①コミュニケーショ
ン、②バランスシート政策、③既存データの活用、④変化する時代の生産性と雇用、⑤FRBのインフ
レ枠組み）の設置を発表し、FRB改革を推進する姿勢を示した。5つのタスクフォースは秋までに何

### Page 4

4/5 
らかの成果を得たうえで、2026 年末までにFRBへ提言をまとめる予定だ。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16
の講演にて、11 月 16 日にサンフランシスコで生産性と雇用に関する公聴会を開催すると述べてお
り、これを含むタスクフォースの進捗状況が開示されるか注目される。

10/12
クリーブランド連銀
ハマック総裁*
インフレ持続の背景として、企業投資の急拡大や資産効果を通じた
消費押し上げによる需要増を指摘した。こうした需要をある程度減
速させる必要性を強調したほか、投資需要を抑制することで雇用を
悪化させずにインフレを抑制できる可能性を指摘した
10/13
ニューヨーク連銀
ウィリアムズ総裁*
年内に追加で1回の利上げを支持した一方、2027年に関しては追加利
上げの必要はないと明言した。この背景として、来年はエネルギー
価格がインフレを押し上げないこと、及び関税の影響が一服するこ
とのほか、足下の米国経済が全体として過熱していないことを指摘
した
10/14
サンフランシスコ連銀
デイリー総裁

「（利上げが）必要となる可能性が高いが、必要な時間はある」と
述べるなど、10月FOMCの利上げに慎重な姿勢を示した。また、予想
を上振れた9月CPIを巡って、 「インフレリスクは依然として高いが、
リスクは現実に起きたわけではない」と、基調的なインフレ動向に
大きな変化が起きていないとの認識を示唆した
10/16 ウォーシュ議長*
AI関連を中心に「投資の急増」が生じていると指摘し、世界経済が
エネルギーショックに対する想定以上の強靭性を示しているとの楽
観的な見方に言及した。一方、先行きの政策決定には「予断を持た
ない」と述べるなど、10月FOMCに関するヒントは与えなかった

研究员：王鹏

第一部分 中国制造业和非制造业 PMI数据表格回顾】

数据来源：银河期货、wind资讯

第二部分  内外需劈叉继续修正 制造业环比走势趋弱

2026年 10月制造业 PMI和大部分分项均下降，制造业 PMI达到 49.3，时隔 1个月再度
回到 50荣枯线以下，预期 50，但符合季节性走势。其中主要原材料购进价格分项下降 4.0，
达到 56.8；出厂价格分项下降 2.3，达到51.7；采购量分项下降 2.1，达到48.9；进口分项
下降 2.2，达到 47.0；在手订单分项下降 1.9，达到 44.1；生产分项下跌 1.9，达到 49.8；
新订单分项下降 1.1，达到49.4，上述是10月制造业 PMI主要的下降分项。原材料库存分项

### Page 2

中国 PMI观察报告 2026年 11月 9日 
宏观数据报告 
第 2 页 共 7 页 
上升 0.2，达到 48.4；内需上升 0.2；供应商时间上升 0.1，达到 50.2；上述三个分项是 10
月制造业 PMI全部上升的分项。整个10月制造业 PMI中，除出厂价格、原材料购进价格、配
送时间和生产活动预期外，绝大部分分项处于50融枯线以下，显示经济处于季节性趋弱的状
态中，但从内外需环比走势看，10月内需继续回升，外需有所下降，经济结构继续改善。 
季节性看，2026年 10月制造业 PMI各分项走势基本符合季节性回落的特征。其中内需
处于历史同期次低；原材料库存处于历史同期最高；新出口订单处于历史同期次高。总结看，
10月制造业PMI表现出宏观经济处于供需双弱、累库存、预期回升的状态，这显示出经济经
历了 8月、9月经济恢复后，10月重回平稳状态，11月和 12月或重回环比回升状态。 
各类企业和不同行业 10月 PMI下滑。各类企业中，大型企、中型和小型企业 PMI均下降，
其中大型企业仍处高位，且处于 50荣枯线以上，中型和小型企业处于 50荣枯线以下。各行
业中，消费品行业、基础材料行业、高技术制造业和装备制造业 PMI均下降。其中高技术制
造业和装备制造业 PMI处于 50荣枯线以上，消费品行业和基础材料行业 PMI处于 50荣枯线
以下，且消费品行业 PMI处于历史同期最低 水平。 
美伊冲突仍是全球经济最大的不确定性，国内商品需求下滑。中国制造业 PMI及进口分
项走势预示中国进出口增速有支撑；工业企业利润增速和 PPI走势正经历拐点。内需低迷的
情况下，国内经济走势将更多依赖政策支撑。预计大宗商品需求内外分化的格局未来将改变。

第三部分  服务业再回低位

2026年 10月非制造业 PMI绝大部分分项下降，时隔 1个月后再度回到 50荣枯线以下。
各分项中，从业人员上升 0.1，达到 46是唯一上升的分项。建筑业分项下降 2.3，达到 48；
新订单分项下降 2.1，达到 44.4；新出口订单分项下降 2，达到 46.5；存货分项下降 1.6，
达到 44.7，上述是下降比较大的分项。 
整体看，10月非制造业 PMI在连续修复后，再回平稳状态，商务活动分项和建筑业分项
再回季节性低位，内需尚可，显示经济恢复之路仍很漫长，但如果地产对经济拖累减弱，确
实是可喜变化。

2344 華邦電(Winbond)

報告日期： 2026/11/06  研 究 員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要  
 3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 為 8.06 元。 受惠記憶體
報價上漲與產品組合改善，第三季毛利率達 70.57% ，預估第
四季營收 839.78 億元， QoQ +2.0% ，毛利率 70.77% 。 
 擴產與 16 奈米轉進帶動 2027 年位元出貨成長。  高雄廠
Module A 新增產能及台中廠 Flash 擴產陸續貢獻，搭配
DDR4 、LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 需求，支持營運成長。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 23.29 ／37.45 元。 AI 伺服器與
企業級 SSD 需求為主要動能， CUBE 及矽電容拓展新應用。

一、3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 營收持續季增 
華邦電為利基型記憶體廠，產品涵蓋客製化記憶體、NOR Flash 及
SLC NAND。3Q26 營收 823.31 億元，QoQ +37.6%、YoY +278.2%；
營業毛利 581.01 億元，毛利率 70.57%；營業利益 437.84 億元，營
業利益率 53.18%；稅後純益 354.22 億元， EPS 7.58 元。營收結構
以客製化記憶體占 58%、Flash 占 32%、邏輯 IC 占 9%。其中，客
製化記憶體平均售價季增近 60%，Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，為
第三季成長主因。展望第四季，公司表示 AI 需求維持強勁，部分
PC 與消費性客戶調整拉貨，整體營運仍將成長，惟增幅低於前幾
季。考量合約價格續升及產品組合改善，預估 4Q26 營收 839.78 億
元，QoQ +2.0%；毛利率 70.77%，營業利益 448.28 億元，稅後 EPS 
8.06 元。

二、高雄廠擴產，支應 DDR4 及 LPDDR4 長期需求 
公司持續供應 DDR3、DDR4 及 LPDDR4，受惠主要記憶體原廠調
整產品配置，以及車用、工業客戶產品生命週期長、替換供應來
源不易，長期供貨需求具支撐。高雄廠 Module A 月產能預計由約
1.5 萬片提高至 2.4 萬至 2.5 萬片，新增產能於 2027 年陸續貢獻，
並提高 16 奈米製程比重，同時增加晶圓投入與單片晶圓位元產
出 。 法人看好擴產搭配製程轉進可帶動 2027 年 DRAM 位元出貨顯
著成長，中信預估增幅超過 100%。本報告採新增產能與製程效益
逐步反映的假設，預估 2027 年營收逐季成長。長期方面，董事會
通過 731 億元資本支出，用於高雄廠 Module B 土建、機電及長交
期設備，規劃月產能約 5 萬至 6 萬片，預計 2029 年下半年開始小
量出貨，並以長期供貨協議支應策略客戶需求。 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：180 元 
      5 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：127.5 元 
     3.4 X 2027 EPS

2

三、AI 帶動 CUBE 與矽電容拓展新應用 
AI 伺服器機櫃內 GPU 、 BMC 、 SmartNIC 及交換器等元件均需 Flash
儲存韌體與設定資料，隨運算及管理架構複雜化，帶動使用顆數
與容量提升。公司預估供應 AI 生態系的 Flash 位元出貨量於 2026
年成長 112%，2027 年再增 67%，其中高容量 NOR Flash 為主要動
能；企業級 SSD 亦增加 DRAM 需求，擴大公司在伺服器供應鏈的
參與 。 法人看好台中廠擴產與高容量產品組合改善 。 新產品方面，
CUBE 主要布局 AI 加速器與推論應用 ， 預計 2027 年下半年增加出
貨；矽電容已小量出貨，既有產能多獲客戶預訂，並透過代工模
式切入 AI 晶片、先進封裝及高速光模組應用，逐步增加產品附加
價值與長期營收來源。  
四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 180 元  
華邦電為全球最大 NOR Flash 廠亦是利基型 DRAM 廠，AI 帶動各
式記憶體需求增加，產能仍供不應求下，獲利將逐季成長。公司
針對邊緣 AI 發展之 CUBE 解決方案將於 2027 年開始貢獻 ， 矽電容
亦為新的業務機會，中長期而言 AI、HPC、IoT、自駕車等新產品
新應用將帶動更多記憶體需求。考量到 1) AI 帶動之記憶體供不應
求將延續至 2028 年、2)華邦電今明年營收獲利創高、3)CUBE、矽
電容具成長潛力，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 5 倍本益比，目標價 180
元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華邦電子於 1987 年九月由華新麗華集團轉投資成立，為自有品牌
IC 設計及製造廠商，並為全世界第五大 DRAM 供應商，且為國內
少數擁有晶圓廠之積體電路設計公司，同時也是全球利基型記憶
體的主要供應商。2008 年公司將邏輯 IC 事業分割成立新唐科技，
新唐產品線以消費性電子 IC 、電腦 IC 兩大產品線為主，並同時擁
有一座 6 吋晶圓廠。華邦電在 NOR Flash 為全球最大供應商，產
品上從中低容量市場拓展至中高容量市場。NOR Flash 主要同業為
旺宏、兆易創新、Infineon。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望

1. 2026 下半年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 2026 年 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 下半年，預估記憶體求維持強勁下，價格持續上漲，唯
因價格基期已高，加上傳統消費性產品追價力道有限，漲幅不若
上半年，不過預估今年隨著價格上漲，加上製程轉換帶動位元出

貨量增加，及新產能貢獻，營收逐季成長，毛利率持續提升，預
估華邦電 2026 年營收 2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%) ， 毛利率 67.17% ，
稅後淨利 1,047.92 億元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

3. 2027 年記憶體更加緊缺，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之記憶體缺貨狀況延續，加上全球新增產能
有限，緊缺情況更加嚴峻，而儘管終端電子產品需求不一，不過
未來 AI 落地後每單位產品之記憶體搭載量持續成長，使得記憶體
位元出貨提升 ，2027 年公司營運仍正向 。DDR4 在全球三大廠逐步
退出下價格自 2Q25 起漲，且因終端電子產品仍有一定需求，價格
將維持高檔，NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 亦因供給端增產有限，價格
逐步回升，其中 SLC Nand 因大廠退出，華邦將成為全球最大供應
商，而應用於 AI Server 的高容量 NOR Flash 持續供不應求，預期
將推升價格上行至 2027 年。華邦電製程技術方面，20 奈米 DRAM
產品仍為主要出貨主力，應用於 DDR3、DDR4、LPDDR 產品，16
奈米製程已開發完成，2027 年將有一定比例轉換至 16 奈米；NOR 
Flash 營收穩定成長 ， NAND Flash 24 奈米及 32 奈米 SLC Nand 今年
2Q 貢獻顯著提升。 因應記憶體需求強勁， 公司 Flash 與 DRAM 擴
產計畫進行中，華邦電 2025 年資本支出為台幣 55 億元，2026 年
將提升至 395 億元，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%，大部分用
於高雄產能及製程提升，少部分用於台中廠製程提升，高雄廠產
能將由現在 15k 擴充至年底的 24k，屆時位元出貨量可望翻倍，同
時啟動高雄 Module B 投資計劃，規劃月產能可達 50k~ 60k，將分
階段擴充，預估 2027 年 1 月動工，2029 年 1 月裝機，2029 年底量
產。2027 年預估 DRAM 、NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 價格持續上漲帶
動營收獲利成長，預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛
利率 72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

4. CUBE 及矽電容為新成長動能 
AI 時代來臨，華邦電長期專注於利基型 DRAM ，針對 AI 將主攻邊
緣運算市場，邊緣運算之記憶體容量不需像 AI Server 那麼高，為
華邦電可著墨之區塊，因此華邦電提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra 
Bandwidth Element 客製化超高頻寬記憶體元件)解決方案，CUBE
具備超低功耗和超高性能、小尺寸、高成本效益，從而大幅提升
系統能力、回應時間和能源效率，以滿足邊緣運算平臺上快速增
長的 AI 應用需求。CUBE 優點在於 1)SoC 推疊在上面，散熱效果
較好、2) 可在 CUBE Die 上做穿孔(TSV)，降低成本、3) 3D 推疊
適合較小的終端裝置。目前華邦電與多家客戶開發 CUBE 方案，
預估 2026 年底開始貢獻，2027 年放量。另外因應 AI 晶片功耗劇
增、瞬間電流波動，以及先進封裝的空間限制，矽電容具有極低
的等效串聯電感與寄生電感，能在極短時間內補償電流，維持電

源完整性，避免電壓驟降，也彌補傳統 MLCC 的不足，華邦電因
客戶需求提供矽電容 foundry 代工，目前產能已滿載，未來 CUBE
及矽電容均為新成長動能。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 毛利率升至 70.57%，EPS 7.58 元 
華邦電 3Q26 營收 823.31 億元(QoQ+37.6%、YoY+278.2%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 742.84 億元(QoQ+43.7%)。受惠客製化記憶體平均售
價季增近 60%、Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，合併毛利率提升至
70.57% ， 營業利益 437.84 億元 ， 歸屬母公司稅後淨利 354.22 億元，
EPS 7.58 元。展望第四季，記憶體合約價格持續上升，惟部分 PC
與消費性客戶調整拉貨，預估營收 839.78 億元(QoQ+2.0%)，毛利
率 70.77%，EPS 8.06 元。

二. 2026 年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 年下半年，AI 伺服器需求強勁，帶動 DDR4、LPDDR4
及高容量 Flash 價格持續上漲，惟第四季部分 PC 與消費性客戶調
整拉貨，營運成長幅度較第三季收斂。預估華邦電 2026 年營收
2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%)，毛利率 67.17%，稅後淨利 1,047.92 億
元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

三. 2027 年擴產與製程轉進帶動成長，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 伺服器與企業級 SSD 需求持續成長，DDR4、
LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 供需維持緊俏，加上高雄廠新增產能
與 16 奈米製程轉進，帶動位元出貨增加，CUBE 及矽電容亦逐步
貢獻營收。預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛利率
72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

### Page 5

### Page 3

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)
06 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Near-term weakness already in the price; maintain Buy and lower TP to NT$286
- Despite near-term margin pressure from softer-than-expected quarterly revenue
and rising depreciation cost, electricity and OPEX, management sees GM
expansion into next year supported by stronger utilization, operating leverage, and
favorable AI-related product mix, in particular substantial upside from wafer

sorting. We lower our earnings projection for 2026/2027 /2028 by 6%/17%/15%
reflecting near-term GM shortfall and potential increasing depreciation cost.
Considering remaining strong AI cycle, we keep our Buy rating but lower our DCF-
derived TP to NT$286 given our lower estimates and concerns on recent
macro/policy uncertainty. Our new TP of NT$286 implies 25x/17x of our 2027 /28
EPS estimates.

### Page 6

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)
06 November 2026 Citi Research
6
King Yuan Electronics Co
Company description
Founded in 1987, King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) mainly provides testing
services for the back-end process of semiconductor production globally. With
more than 30 years of industry experience, KYEC has built up firm
relationships with several leading global IC design/IDM customers. Its
headquarters and production facilities are both located in Taiwan, with sales

offices in North America, Japan, and Singapore.
Investment strategy
We rate KYEC as Buy. As a key testing service provider for AI GPU and ASIC
products, KYEC is well positioned to benefit from the significant AI growth
opportunity in the years to come. Currently, KYEC is the major final testing
service provider for its major customer's AI GPU, and we expect KYEC to
continue to secure its dominant share in its customer's upcoming AI GPU
product lines due to its specialty in burn-in testing, smooth product delivery

capability, and its strong business partnership with its customer. We are also
positive over the growth outlook of its smartphone business thanks to the
integration of AI functionality, leading to a better replacement cycle. We
believe KYEC's major customer in smartphone will benefit from an improved
product mix, which will result in longer testing times for the flagship SoC.
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology.

Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of 4%, a risk premium of 5%,
and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a
3.0% interest rate on its debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume
terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually
improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares
would trade at 2027 /28E P/B of 4x/4x and 2027 /28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line
with its global peers.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-demand for global
smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related
products; 3) slower-than-expected demand recovery in the automotive and
base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Alchip Technologies Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Second big customer won, with
revenue upside from major
customer; OW
Alchip confirmed winning a second big customer from the US.
Given strong demand from a major customer, it is possible for
Alchip's 2027 revenue to double from 2026 and double again in
2028. We think the stock should react positively to the new
customer win and better 2027-28 outlook.
Major surprise – winning a second customer: Previously, in Alchip Technologies

Ltd: Solid 2027 and 2028 outlook with potential catalysts; OW (11 Oct 2026), we
highlighted that we believe Alchip is a leading candidate for Google’s TPU KGD back-
end design opportunity. While that remains the case, Alchip’s pursuit of another US
HPC customer opportunity has borne fruit earlier. Based on management's
comments, we believe this second customer is one of the major US CSPs; it could
contribute US$1bn revenue in 2027.
Updates on major customer (AWS): Alchip management expect 4Q26 revenue to

trend higher vs. 3Q26. When answering questions regarding AI demand uncertainty,
Alchip doesn’t see any signs of slowdown from the customer side and expects
revenue from its major project (3nm AWS Trainium3) to grow >100% in 2027.
Meanwhile, the next-generation project (2nm AWS Trainium4) will tape out soon;
there should be a small revenue contribution from this in 4Q27. The company
continues to believe that it is the only design service provider for the 2nm project,

given the value it adds for the customer in terms of time to market.
We raise our revenue forecast: We update our revenue breakdown model. We
think 2026 revenue should end up at ~US$2.3bn-US$2.4bn. Management thinks our
US$4.5bn to US$5.0bn revenue outlook for 2027  (doubling from 2026) is
reasonable. We think the company can further double its revenue in 2028, thanks to
AWS Trainium4 production, and revenue streams from the second and third US CSP

Stronger revenue   guidance for 2027 and 2028
New customer contribution: We raised our 2027 and 2028 revenue forecasts given the
second major customer's design win confirmation. The company guided that the
contribution could start from 2Q27 and that revenue scale could reach US$1bn. We
currently expect revenue from this customer to increase a further 20% in 2028 with a
full-year contribution.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Earnings estimate revisions
We cut our 2026 earnings estimate 20%; 2027e and 2028e EPS virtually unchanged:
The cut to 2026 revenue and                  earnings forecasts is mostly driven by the revenue
recognition deferred to 2027 and lower 3Q26 gross margin, which slightly missed the
company's 20% target. We raise 2027-28 revenue forecasts given the new customer
design win, but also lower our margin assumptions for both years –  we think this new

project would also carry a dilutive margin profile because the customer could be a major
US CSP or HPC. We also slightly lower our non-US CSP customer revenue contribution
forecasts for both 2027/2028 as we believe the company will prioritize foundry capacity
for CSP customers. Net,  our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates change <1%.

Price target remains unchanged at NT$5,888
We continue to derive our base case value, which is also our price target, from a residual
income model. Our cost of equity of 10.4% (beta of 1.4, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and
risk-free rate of 2.0%) remains unchanged, as do our intermediate growth rate of  12.5%
and terminal growth rate of 4%.
Our bear case value of NT$2,465 and bull case value of NT$6,880 are also unchanged.

MS Rating
▪ We continue to believe that AI ASICs will
outgrow AI GPUs in the coming years, and
Alchip is one of the few ASIC design service
providers that can deal with increasingly
complex designs (at 2nm) with good
production/design track records for large
leading edge chips.
▪ AWS's announcement of its 3nm
Trainium3 chip matches the timing of
Alchip's 3nm project win. The growing
number of use cases for Trainium (e.g.,
Apple's adoption) bodes well for Alchip's

revenue growth in 2026.
▪ We believe Alchip has strong potential to
win Trainium4.
▪ Our price target implies 26x our 2027e
EPS, which we ﬁnd undemanding vs. our
>100% revenue CAGR for 2025-28e.

NT$6,880.00
30x 2027e EPS

AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate, amid substantial end demand.
Alchip's leading-edge tape-out numbers
increase signiﬁcantly, with some high-proﬁle
project wins at 3nm or even 2nm. Alchip is
able to secure enough foundry capacity
(front-end wafers and back-end packaging)
and substrates for all of its leading-edge
turnkey production. Alchip gradually gains
market share within the design service
market, amid continued proliferation of

custom chip demand.
NT$5,888.00
26x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate amid strong end demand,
resulting in steady growth in tape-outs and
volume. There might be a slowdown in
Alchip's Chinese customers' design activity
for leading-edge chips as a result of US
export controls; we believe this would be
largely offset by ASIC demand strength
from non-China regions.
NT$2,465.00
11x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects lack concrete

end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs
and mass production. Alchip's leading-edge
project pipeline deteriorates, or some
customers turn directly to TSMC. AI GPU's
dominance in AI computing compresses the
market share gain potential for overall ASIC,
with customers starting to slow their
custom silicon developments - and therefore
ASIC procurement plans.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Novatek  | Asia Pacific
Consumer Demand Softens as
Cost Pressure Builds
3Q growth led by smartphones: Revenue was NT$30.97bn, +8% Q/Q and +26% Y/
Y, 7% above MSe. Net income was NT$6.08bn, -4% Q/Q and +66% Y/Y, 2% above
MSe, with EPS of NT$9.99. Smartphone restocking lifted sales, but GM fell 1.53ppt
Q/Q to 40.89% on a lower SoC mix and a lag in cost pass-through. Lower opex
supported a 9.6% Q/Q rise in operating profit; net income declined in the absence of

2Q's NT$860mn one-off investment gain.
4Q revenue and margins seen declining sequentially: Management guided for
revenue of NT$28.0-29.2bn, down 6-10% Q/Q, with GM of 37-39% and OpM of 17-
19%, assuming NT$/US$32. Large DDICs should face the largest sequential revenue
drop, particularly from notebooks, followed by SoC; small/medium DDICs should
decrease slightly despite stronger Chinese OLED handset orders. Lower-cost
inventory depletion, product mix and higher memory KGD costs will likely weigh on

margins. Prices have been adjusted across SoC and DDIC products, but management
still sees higher memory prices constraining consumer demand into 2027.
New products offer growth, with cost risks unresolved: Automotive eDP TDDI is
scheduled for mass production in 4Q26, while touch-enabled notebook products are
scheduled to ramp through 2027. OLED solutions for IT applications and higher-
performance handset products broaden opportunities, although smartphone OLED

DDIC shipments are expected to decline Y/Y in 2026. Management expects new
products to support 2027 revenue growth but provided no quantified target or
assurance of flat Y/Y margins. Further foundry and OSAT price increases remain
under negotiation, leaving earnings dependent upon product mix and the pace of
cost pass-through.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Novatek (3034.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity constant at 9.8% (2.0%
risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta), payout ratio of 80%, medium-term growth rate of
6.1% and terminal growth rate assumption of 3.4%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Better-than-expected smartphone sell-through.
n Better-than-expected sell-through of foldable products.

Risks to Downside
n Intensified price competition in TDDI, driving Novatek's gross margin below its corpo-
rate average.
n Slower-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Slower sell-through of foldable products.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

3Q26 Review: EV 판매 증가의 역풍 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 160,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 95,600원(10/30)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1%yoy), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%yoy, OPM 5.8%), 지배이익 1.29조원(-9.8%yoy)을 기록, 도매 판
매 증가로 인한 매출 증가에도 불구하고 인센티브/원자재 비용 증가로 인해 
컨센서스를 하회하는 실적 쇼크를 기록 
 영업이익 증감 요인: 인센티브&가격 -7,270억원, 믹스 -4,650억원, 재료비 
-3,680억원으로 총 1.56조원 비용 발생. 증가 요인은 판매량 +4,420억원, 
환율 +5,290억원, 기타비용 절감 +4,130억원으로 1.38조원 상쇄. 인센티
브로 인한 비용 증가 예상외로 컸는데 EV 판매량 증가 영향. 기말환율 하락
으로 판매보증충당금은 전년비 9,254억원 감소 
 전동화 믹스 증가: 글로벌 xEV 소매 판매 30만대(+47%yoy), 판매비중 
36.2%(+9.8%p yoy). HEV 17.2만대(+46%yoy), 텔루라이드, 스포티지 
HEV 공급 확대로 3분기 미국 HEV 판매 +118%yoy 기록. EV 12.3만대
(+75%yoy), 서유럽 EV 판매는 +163%yoy 기록 
 4분기 전망: 중동 분쟁 지속으로 원가 부담의 급격한 개선은 어려울 전망. 
연초 CID에서 제시한 연간 판매 목표는 달성 가능할 것으로 보이지만 기존 
수익성 목표를 하회할 전망. 중장기적으로 상품 경쟁력 강화, 지역별 파워트
레인 대응 전략 유지, 내부 원가 및 체질 개선으로 외부 비용 부담 상쇄를 
추진할 계획. 중동 분쟁 완화에 따른 비용 부담 해소와 HEV 비중 확대를 통
한 수익성 개선은 2027년 시점이 될 것으로 예상 
 단기 방향 전환 어려울 듯: 판매 호조에도 불구하고 저가형 EV 판매 비중 
증가함에 따라 수익성이 악화되는 모습이 3분기에도 재확인. 글로벌 수요 
변화를 감안했을 때, EV 시장 선점이 매우 중요한 것은 사실이지만 단기 수
익성 부담 커지고 있는 점 우려됨. 중국산 EV의 글로벌 확산, 중동 전쟁 장
기화에 따른 원가 부담 증대가 연말까지는 영향을 미칠 것. 동사에 대한 투
자의견 매수와 목표주가 16만원 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
현대차 (005380)

26.3Q Review: 이제 올라가야 할 때 Company Brief

본업 Review: 26.3Q 매출액 45.1조원, 영업이익 2.6조원  
현대차는 26.3Q 매출액 45.1원 (-3.4% YoY, -8.3% QoQ), 영업이익 2.6조원 (+5.5% 
YoY, -6.1% QoQ , +5.9% OPM ) 등의 실적을 시현했다 [iM증권 추정치 매출액: 
45.3조원, 영업이익: 2.4조원]. 매출액은 시장 컨센서스에 부합했고, 영업이익의 경우 
시장 컨센서스였던 2.4조원을 소폭 상회했다. 주요 구성 요소 별 내용은 아래와 같다.  
① P: 26.3Q HEV 판매 대수는 16.6만대를 기록하며 전사 판매 대비 비중 
18.2% 달성 (+18.8% YoY, -11.1% QoQ). 미국의 경우 국내 공장 생산 
감소에 따른 수출 부진으로 도매 판매는 소폭 감소했으나, 판매 비중은 
25.2%로 전반적인 믹스는 직전 분기와 유사. 26.3Q 평균환율이 직전 분기 
대비 크게 하락했으나, 전년 동기 기저 탓에 오히려 + 환율 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매 대수는 91.8만대로 전년 동기 대비 11.5% 감소 (이하 
YoY 기준, 미국: -10.0%, 유럽: -18.1% 국내: -27.2% 인도: + 18.3%). 
26.2Q 안전 공업 화재에 이어 26.3Q에 발생한 파업의 영향으로 생산 
대수는 감소. 국내 공장에서의 생산 차질은 내수 판매에 더해 수출 감소, 즉 
전체적인 판매 축소로 연결됨. 26.4Q부터 생산 관련 노이즈는 발생하지 
않을 전망. 이에 더해 아반떼, 투싼 등의 신차 판매 효과를 통한 반등 가능.  
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 직전 분기와 유사한 수준으로 발생한 것으로 파악 
(25.2Q 0.8조원, 25.3Q 1.8조원, 25.4Q 1.5조원, 26.1Q 0.8조원, 26.2Q 
0.9조원). 이란 사태에 따른 주요 원자재 가격 상승 압력은 계속 존재하나, 
내부 원가 절감 정책으로 약 5,000억원의 원가 상승분을 상쇄. 현대차는 
26.4Q 원가 절감 정책의 효과가 더 크게 나타날 것이라고 언급. 26.3Q 
기말 환율이 전년 동기 대비 급락함에 따라 판매보층충당부채전입액 또한 
감소 (26.3Q 매출원가, 판매관리비 각 전년 동기 대비 1.7%, 19.2% 감소). 
금융 부문의 경우 26.3Q 매출액 9.1조원 (+27.8% YoY) , 영업이익 4,710억원 (-
18.2% YoY, -37.9%) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 완성차 판매가 전반적으로 
위축되었음에도 외형 성장을 이어갔으나, 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 등 전반적인 
조달 관련 비용이 증가했다. 일부 EV를 중심으로 중고차 가격이 소폭 하락한 점 또한 
HCA 등 주요 부문 이익 감소의 배경이다. 26.3Q 현대차 순이익은 1,932억원을 
기록했는데, 이는 영업외손익이 전년 동기 789억원에서 229억원으로 감소했기 
때문이다. 현대차는 주요 환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실 증가의 영향이라고 밝혔다. 
1) 생산 단에서의 노이즈가 연속해 발생하며 내수 판매 및 수출 감소가 불가피했으나, 
2) 원자재 가격 상승 영향을 내부 원가 절감 정책을 통해 상쇄했고, 3) 주요 
평균환율이 하락했으나 기말환율 또한 급락하며 판매보증충당부채전입액 감소 효과가 
나타나 시장 기대를 소폭 상회하는 수익성이 관찰되었다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
현대차는 2026년 도매 판매 가이던스를 416만대에서 400만대로 하향 조정했다. 
26.3Q 누적 도매 판매 대수는 288만대이기에 하향 조정은 불가피했다. 단 신규 
가이던스에 따르면 26.4Q 판매 목표는 약 110만대 수준이라는 것인데, 아반떼, 투싼, 
주요 모델 페이스리프트, GV80 HEV 등의 신차 효과가 얼마나 나타날지가 관건일 
것이다 (이하 분기 도매 판매, 25.1Q 100.1만대, 25.2Q 105.7만대, 25.3Q 103.8만대).

현대차 (005380)
2
미래 사업 업데이트: 자율주행은 계획대로, Atlas는 조금씩 변신중
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 특별히 SDV~Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트는 확인되지
않았으나 여러 매체를 통해 파악된 현대차그룹의 미래 사업 진행 상황은 다음과 같다.
① SDV/자율주행: 데이터 플라이 휠에 대한 중요성 강조. 27년 말부터 광주
권역 자율주행 실증 사업에 투입되는 차량은 Atria AI 활용. SDV 플랫폼이
적용된 차량 양산 시작은 29년. 양산 SDV 출하량 증가함에 따라 29년부터
GPU 5 만개 이상으로 구성되는 새만금 데이터센터를 자체 운영을 목표함.
박민우 포티투닷 사장 겸 AVP 본부장이 부임한 이후 Tesla, nVIDIA 출신의
개발 인력을 충원 중인 상황. 26.05 그랜저 페이스리프트 모델에 플레오스
탑재 시작. 26.09부터 판매되고 있는 신형 아반떼에도 플레오스 탑재 완료.

② Boston Dynamics: 10.8 Rohit Prasad를 CEO로 선임. Rohit Prasad은
아마존에서 Alex 개발에 중추적인 역할을 수행했던 인물. 현대차그룹에서
직접 하드웨어 개발 및 양산을 챙기고, 소프트웨어에 역량을 높이겠다는
행보로 판단. 26.10 Atlas의 신규 덱스트러스를 공개했는데, 기존 제품 대비
자유도가 크게 증가 (7DoF→14DoF). 순수 직구동 방식을 여전히
고수함.10.27 Boston Dynamics가 주요 부품 업체에게 Atlas 1만대 물량의
부품 PO를 곧 발송할 것이라는 기사가 보도됨. 28년까지 연 3만대 CAPA의
공장 완공이라는 기존 계획 대비 양산 및 개발 속도가 빨라진다는 시그널임.
현대차그룹의 미래 사업 업데이트를 또 찾아볼 수 있을 가장 빠른 이벤트는 CES
2027이다. 다만 CES 2027에는 현대차가 아닌 그룹사가 참여함에 따라 해당 행사에서

유의미한 업데이트 내용은 찾아보기 힘들 것이며 이에 중장기 관점의 접근이 필요하다.
시장의 우려가 반영된 주가 하락: 빨라질 속도와 올라올 본업에 주목
현대차 주가는 26.2H 접어들며 크게 하락하고 있고, CES 2026 전후의 상승폭을 사실상
모두 반납했다 (연초 기준 -1.3%, 26.2H 이후 -41.7%). 또한 최근 시장 반등
흐름에서도 사실상 소외되고 있다. 현대차를 바라보는 투자자들의 우려는 아래와 같다.
첫째, CID 전후로 Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트가 너무 부족하다는 것이다. 26.08
현대차 CID 행사를 앞두고 많은 투자자들은 빅테크 업체들의 지분 투자 및 Atlas
개발과 관련해 새로운 내용을 기대한 것이 사실이다. 이런 상황에서 유럽, 내수 등 주요
지역 에서의 판매 업황 또한 좋지 않다 보니, 시장의 실망은 더욱 컸다. 전술한 것과
같이 CES 2027에 현대차가 참여하지 않는다는 점에서 내년에 대한 기대도 크지 않다.

둘째, 내수와 유럽 업황이 그룹사 대비 너무 좋지 못하다는 점이다. 26.3Q 현대차 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 27.2%, 18.1% 감소했다. 반면 26.3Q 기아 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 2.2%, 18.7% 증가했다. 특히 유럽에서의 부진은
EV 라인업이 상대적으로 빈약하고, 중국 업체와의 경쟁이 심화되고 있다는 구조적
문제에서 출발하기에, 투자자들은 26년을 넘어 27, 28년의 성장까지 우려하고 있다
단 우리는 Boston D ynamics의 Atlas는 양산이라는 방향성이 설정된지 오래되지
않았다는 점을 기억해야 한다. 초기 단계부터 양산을 위해 개발된 Tesla Optimus 대비
개발 속도가 빠를 수는 없다. 1~2만대 수준의 데모 제품이 확보되고, 이를 바탕으로
데이터 수집이 일정 부분 확보되는 이후부터 관련 사업 확대 속도는 빨라 질것이다.

취약 지역 판매 업황의 경우에도 내수를 시작으로 점진적으로 개선될 것이다. 생산 차질
외에도 Tesla 및 중국 OEM들의 시장 침투가 우려됨은 분명하지만, 주력 차종 중에서도
국내 판매 볼륨이 큰 아반떼와 투싼의 신차 효과는 분명 기대되는 지점이다. 유럽에서의
반등은 시간이 필요할 것이나, 일단 아이오닉 3가 26.4Q부터 본격적으로 판매될
예정이다. 단 이는 큰 변동 없이 IAA가 2028년부터 시행된다는 전제에 근거하고 있다.
현대차 목표주가 600,000원 유지: 여전한 Multiple 할증 요인
현대차 목표주가를 600,000원으로 유지한다. 26년 EPS 기준 6~8배 수준의 P/E
Multiple을 부여받고 있는 Peer Group들은 중국 시장 M/S 축소로 수익성이 급락하고
있고, 미래 사업 경쟁력은 여전히 전무하다. 현대차의 Multiple 할증 요인은 충분하다.

그림16. IAA 법안 개요 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

그림17. 유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 추이 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

IAA는 EU판 IRA로 볼 수 있음. 현대차그룹의 경우 현대차 (터키, 체코)와 기아 (슬로바키아) 모두 유렵 현지
생산 라인을 이미 확보했고, 현대모비스를 중심으로 한 부품 자급률 비중 이미 높음. 또한 주요 부품에 있어
FTA 체결국의 원산지를 EU산과 동일하게 적용하는 규정 존재. 실제 발효까지 큰 변수가 없다면 국내 완성차
업체의 경우 구조적으로 수혜 클 것. 단 중국 업체들에 대한 명시적 배제 규정이 없다는 점은 한계 중 하나. 
IAA 주요 조항 주요 내용
적용 대상
공공조달 차량, 공적 지원(구매 보조금 등)을 받는 차량, 법인차가 대상임. 전기
차, PHEV, 수소차에 적용됨. 2028년부터 회원국은 무공해 및 저공해이면서 EU
산인 차량이 아니면 법인차를 지원할 수 없음. 판매 금지가 아니라 수요 측 혜택
을 역내 생산과 연동하는 구조
역내 부품 요건
1단계(발효 6개월 후)는 EU 조립, 배터리 제외 부품가치 70% EU산, 셀 포함
배터리 핵심부품 3개 EU산을 요구함. 2단계(1단계 3년 후)는 배터리 핵심부품
5개(셀, 양극재,BMS 포함)와 전동 파워트레인·전장 가치 50% EU산이 추가됨.
소형차 특례
전장 4.2m 이하 전기 승용차가 대상임. EU 조립에 더해 부품가치 70%와
배터리 핵심부품 3개 중 하나만 충족하면 됨. 충족 시 CO2 규제상 1.3대로 인정
됨(2025~34년).
FDI 조건 1억 유로 초과 투자가 대상이며, 지분 49% 상한, EU 인력 50% 이상, EU산 투 입재 30% 이상 등의 조건이 붙음.
FT A·관세동맹국 예외
FT A나 관세동맹을 맺은 국가의 부품은 EU산과 동등한 부품으로 인정되며,
정부조달협정(GPA) 가입국도 일부 조항에 포함됨. 단 최종 조립국까지 인정되
는지는 불명확하며, 조문상으로는 EU, EEA 역내 조립만 인정되는 것으로 해석
됨. 따라서 한국산 부품은 EU산으로 인정 가능하나, 한국이나 튀르키예에서 조
립한 완성차는 요건 미충족 가능성이 높음.
FT A 예외 배제 조건
집행위는 위임법으로 특정국을 동등 대상에서 제외할 수 있음. 해당국이 EU 기업
에 내국민 대우를 하지 않는 경우, 공급망 의존도나 공급 안보 위협이 있는 경우,
해당 협정의 예외 조항에 해당하는 경우가 배제 사유임. 중국은 FT A가 없어 애초
에 대상이 아님.
2020 2021 2022 2023
대당 생산원가 30,683 32,029 35,079 38,140
대당 판매가격 34,404 27,557 31,244 33,560
1인당 인건비 70,981 74,904 78,348 88,198
통상적으로 중국 OEM의 생산 원가 (중국 생산 가정)은 글로벌 OEM 대비 30% 낮다고 추정됨. BYD의 경우 돌핀 등과 같
은 C 세그먼트 차량에서의 유럽 수익성이 낮지만, A~B 세그먼트 급의 돌핀 서프의 경우 매우 높은 수익성 시현. 이는 유럽
시장 내의 A~B 세그먼트 경쟁 강도가 낮다는 점에서 기인. 즉 100% 수준의 관세가 부과되지 않는다면 BYD의 가격 경쟁
력은 유지되는 것. 따라서 EU는 2,000유로 수준의 중국 OEM의 대당 이익을 상쇄하기 위해 이에 준하는 수준으로 지급되
는 EV 구매 보조금 및 법인 차량 세제혜택을 검토 중인 것이고, 이를 IAA에 담아낼지가 핵심.
유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 및 판매가격 추이

그림23. 26.3Q 국내 완성차 실적 Summary

자료: iM증권 리서치본부

현대차 미주: 펠리세이드 리콜 이슈 존재하나, 메타플랜트 및 기존 앨러배마 공
장 HEV 생산을 통한 시장 진출 속도 빨라질 것으로 전망
인도: GM 탈레가온 공장 인수를 통한 생산량 확대에 나설 예정이고, 이
를 통한 인도 내수 수요 대응 및 글로벌 신흥 시장 진출 또한 목표
유럽: 기아 대비 EV 라인업의 폭에 열위에 있으나, 캐스퍼 EV, EV 3를
통해 EV 수요 회복 국면을 앞두고 있는 유럽 시장 대응에 나설 예정
기아 미주: 텔룰라이드 HEV 출시를 통해 Mid-Size  HEV 수요 대응 이뤄지
고 있음. 스포티지 및 셀토스 등 HEV를 통한 기존 차급 대응 강화 전망
인도: 2030년까지 10개 차종 출시 목표하고 있고, 이를 통해 2030년
41만대 도매 판매, 시장 점유율 7.6% 달성 가이던스를 제시함
유럽: EV2 출시를 통한 EV 라인업 완성도 높임. 소형 해치백 차량과 신
규 HEV 차량 출시, 그리고 PBV 판매 본격화를 통한 초과 성장 목표

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

Document 253: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% Y oY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% Y oY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다. 
목표주가 (유지) 2,000,000원  
현재가 (10/21) 1,417,000원

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Document 290: Anthropic IPO遭疯抢！认购需求数倍超额，目标估值1.4万亿美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO遭疯抢！认购需求数倍超额，目标估值1.4万亿美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Anthropic 即将进⾏的 IPO 引发市场热捧，认购需求已达数倍。公司计划通过 IPO 及私募配
售最⾼募资 904 亿美元，有望刷新史上最⼤规模上市纪录。虽然其 1.4 万亿美元的估值⽬标
略低于此前预期，但在机构锚定需求的强⼒驱动下，市场反应依旧极其热烈。
摘要：
Anthropic 的⾸次公开募股正成为市场焦点。据彭博 11 ⽇知情⼈⼠透露，该公司 IPO 认购需求已
达可售股份的数倍，有望创下史上最⼤规模上市纪录。
据彭博报道，本轮超额认购主要由所谓 " 锚定规模 " 的认购意向驱动。Anthropic 及其联合创始⼈
寻求通过 IPO 及私募配售合计募资最⾼ 904 亿美元，若与 SpaceX 未来 IPO 相⽐，此次发⾏规模将
超越其历史纪录，成为有史以来规模最⼤的上市交易。

在估值⽅⾯， Anthropic ⽬前将完全摊薄估值⽬标定为约 1.4 万亿美元，低于部分投资者此前的预
期⽔平。此次上市由⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利联席主承销，另有 20 家银⾏参与，股票将在纳斯达克全
球精选市场以 "ANT" 为代码交易。
锚定需求驱动超额认购
据彭博援引知情⼈⼠称， Anthropic IPO 的超额认购主要来⾃机构投资者的⼤额锚定意向，⽽⾮
分散的零散需求。
此次发⾏若顺利完成， IPO 与私募配售合计募资规模最⾼可达 904 亿美元，这⼀数字将使其⼀举
超越 SpaceX 的预期 IPO 规模，成为全球有史以来最⼤规模的⾸发上市交易。知情⼈⼠同时表
⽰， IPO 的具体细节仍可能发⽣变化。
Anthropic 将此次 IPO 的完全摊薄估值⽬标设定在约 1.4 万亿美元，低于市场此前部分投资者的预
期落点。彭博报道指出，部分公司在正式启动路演时倾向于采取保守定价策略，以期通过强劲
需求推⾼最终定价，并为上市后的⼆级市场交易奠定坚实基础。
路演造势， Amodei 与摩根⼤通 CEO 同台

为进⼀步提振 IPO 需求， Anthropic 正密集开展⾯向潜在投资者的路演活动。据知情⼈⼠透露，
公司计划于周五举办⼀场⽹络直播活动，届时 CEO Dario Amodei 将与摩根⼤通 CEO Jamie
Dimon 同台对话，向市场传递公司发展前景与战略愿景。
在需求⾼涨背景下，市场围绕此次 IPO 也出现了多种衍⽣操作。部分获得 IPO 配额的投资者正将
所持份额转让给其他潜在买⽅，借此收取额外费⽤并分散上市⾸⽇的交易⻛险；另有持有上市
前股份的投资者通过特殊⽬的载体（ SPV ）向买⽅营销其股权。
Anthropic 对此未⽴即回应置评请求。

### Page 2

北京大学国民经济研究中心 
内容提要 
2026 年 10 月份，CPI 同比增长 0.7%，较前月下降 0.2 个百分点，供给充足食品价格继
续下行，居民消费价格整体运行平稳，震荡前行。10 月能源价格继续上涨，对当前 CPI 增
速形成主要的拉升因素。 
2026 年 10 月份，PPI 同比增长 5.0%，较前月上涨 1.2 个百分点，上游原材料价格继续
上涨，尤其能源价格的大幅上涨，推动工业品价格增速继续上行。一方面，8 月以来的美伊
战争加剧，国际能源价格再次上涨；另一方面，促消费叠加整治“反内卷”式竞争，耐用消
费品价格增速明显上涨。 
10 月通信工具价格同比增长 16.1%，较9 月上涨 1.8 个百分点， 对本月CPI 增速形成拉
升因素；交通工具用能源价格同比增长 20.2%，较 9 月上涨 5.3 个百分点，亦对 CPI 增速形
成主要的拉升因素。与外贸进出口、PPI、工业企业利润等指标相契合，通信工具价格持续
上涨， 价格增速高位运行， 主要受科技大爆发， 终端需求旺盛推动的价格不断上涨， 是经济
结构调整的阶段性结果。

正  文 
需求推动，通信工具价格继续上行 
2026 年 10 月份，CPI 同比增长 0.7%，较前月下降 0.2 个百分点，供给充足食品价格继
续下行，居民消费价格整体运行平稳，震荡前行。10 月能源价格继续上涨，对当前 CPI 增
速形成主要的拉升因素。

### Page 3

北京大学国民经济研究中心 
本月食品价格同比下降 2.9%，降幅较前月下降 1.2 个百分点，是导致本月 CPI 增速小
幅下滑的主要因素。10 月果蔬供给充足， 鲜果、 鲜菜价格同比下降3.8%、16.9%， 降幅分别
较 9 月扩大 2.3、8.8 个百分点，是拉低本月 CPI 增速的主要因素之一。 
10 月通信工具价格同比增长 16.1%，较9 月上涨 1.8 个百分点， 对本月CPI 增速形成拉
升因素；交通工具用能源价格同比增长 20.2%，较 9 月上涨 5.3 个百分点，亦对 CPI 增速形
成主要的拉升因素。与外贸进出口、PPI、工业企业利润等指标相契合，通信工具价格持续
上涨， 价格增速高位运行， 主要受科技大爆发， 终端需求旺盛推动的价格不断上涨， 是经济
结构调整的阶段性结果。

图 3 CPI 食品与非食品当月同比增速（%） 图 4 CPI 食品与非食品环比增速（%）

食品项中， 从同比看， 食品价格增长-2.9%，较前月下降 1.2 个百分点，供给充足使得食
品价格同比继续负增长。总体而言， 食品项八大类价格同比分化 （1 涨、7 跌、0 持平 ），其
中鲜菜价格同比幅度最大（-16.9%）；较上月同比变动分化（3 正、4 负、1 不变），其中鲜
菜价格同比变动最显著（-8.8%）。

能源价格波动，PPI 增速上行 
2026 年 10 月份，PPI 同比增长 5.0%，较前月上涨 1.2 个百分点，上游原材料价格继续
上涨，尤其能源价格的大幅上涨，推动工业品价格增速继续上行。一方面，8 月以来的美伊
战争加剧，国际能源价格再次上涨；另一方面，促消费叠加整治“反内卷”式竞争，耐用消
费品价格增速明显上涨。 
8 月以来的美伊战争加剧，使得国际原油价格上涨，10 月 PPI 中，石油和天然气开采
业、石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业价格分别同比增长35.0%、29.9%，较 9 月上涨 10.5、8.3 个
百分点， 本月能源及相关工业品价格增速继续明显上涨，是推动本月 PPI 增速上涨的主要因
素。

资源争夺叠加供需不匹配问题缓解，2026 年价格增速或有上行 
CPI 方面，深入整治 “反内卷” 式竞争或能缓解供给相对过剩的问题， 同时受 “稳增长、
促消费” 政策影响，叠加 2025 年的低基数效应， 或推动2026 年 CPI 增速上行。经济内生动
力不足， 国内经济压力依然较大， 就业、 收入预期并未发生明显改变， 居民有效需求不足问
题掣肘，抑制居民消费价格短期快速大幅上涨，但若中东战争的影响持续扩大， 或加速通胀
率的上行。综合预计 2026 年 CPI 增速或温和上涨。 
PPI 方面，一方面，国内外环境进一步复杂多变，能源、矿产争夺加剧，或推动上游原
材料价格上涨，政策调整， “依法依规治理企业低价无序竞争”， 叠加低基数效应， 拉动2026
年 PPI 增速上行； 另一方面，中国国内经济压力犹存， 企业内生动力不足， 工业有效需求有
待刺激， 房地产市场产能去化犹在， 经济结构调整继续， 抑制需求端扩张。 但若中东战争的
影响持续扩大，或产生输入性通胀，工业品价格上涨或加速。综合预计 2026 年 PPI 增速或
震荡上行。

北京大学国民经济研究中心 
北京大学国民经济研究中心简介： 
北京大学国民经济研究中心成立于 2004 年，挂靠在北京大学经济学院。依托北京大学，重点研
究领域包括中国经济波动和经济增长、宏观调控理论与实践、经济学理论、中国经济改革实践、转轨
经济理论和实践前沿课题、政治经济学、西方经济学教学研究等。同时，本中心密切跟踪宏观经济与
政策的重大变化， 将短期波动和长期增长纳入一个综合性的理论研究框架， 以独特的观察视角去解读，
把握宏观趋势、剖析数据变化、理解政策初衷、预判政策效果。 
中心的研究取得了显著的成果， 对中国的宏观经济政策产生了较大影响。 其中最具有代表性的成
果有： （1）推动了中国人口政策的调整。中心主任苏剑教授从 2006 年开始就呼吁中国应该立即彻底
放弃计划生育政策， 并转而鼓励生育。 （2） 关于宏观调控体系的研究： 中心提出了包括市场化改革、
供给管理和需求管理政策的三维宏观调控体系。 （3） 关于宏观调控力度的研究：2017 年 7 月， 本中
心指出中国的宏观调控应该严防用力过猛， 这一建议得到了国务院主要领导的批示， 也与三个月后十
九大报告中提出的“宏观调控有度”的观点完全一致。（4）关于中国经济目标增速的成果。2013 年，
刘伟、 苏剑经过缜密分析和测算， 认为中国每年只要有6.5%的经济增速就可以确保就业。 此后不久，
这一增速就成为中国政府经济增长速度的基准目标。 最近几年中国经济的实践也证明了他们的这一测
算结果的精确性。 （5）供给侧研究。刘伟和苏剑教授是国内最早研究供给侧的学者，他们在 2007 年
就开始在 《经济研究》 等杂志上发表关于供给管理的学术论文。 （6） 新常态研究。 刘伟和苏剑合作的
论文“新常态下的中国宏观调控”（ 《经济科学》2014 年第 4 期） 是研究中国经济新常态的第一篇学术
论文。 苏剑和林卫斌还研究了发达国家的新常态。 （7） 刘伟和苏剑主编的 《寻求突破的中国经济》 被
译成英文、韩文、俄文、日文、印地文 5 种文字出版。（8）北京地铁补贴机制研究。2008 年，本课
题组受北京市财政局委托设计了北京市地铁运营的补贴机制。该机制从 2009 年 1 月 1 日开始被使
用，直到现在。 
中心出版物有： （1） 《原富》杂志。 《原富》是一个月度电子刊物，由北京大学国民经济研究中
心主办，目的是以最及时、最专业、 最全面的方式呈现本月国内外主要宏观经济大事并对重点事件进
行专业解读。 （2） 《中国经济增长报告》 （年度报告） 。该报告主要分析中国经济运行中存在的中长
期问题，从 2003 年开始已经连续出版 14 期，是相关年度报告中连续出版年限最长的一本， 被教育部
列入其年度报告资助计划。（3）系列宏观经济运行分析和预测报告。本中心定期发布关于中国宏观
经济运行的系列分析和预测报告，尤其是本中心的预测报告在预测精度上在全国处于领先地位。

Document 367: India_ A middle class too small to supplant China’s economic role (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/India_ A middle class too small to supplant China’s economic role (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
At a time when Chinese consumption is slowing significantly, could India become a driver of global consumption? For now, this
prospect presents considerable difficulties, with many obstacles to overcome. Despite dynamic growth and the Indian economy’s
expanding share of global output (8.2% of worldwide GDP in 2025), the middle-class consumer population remains modest and is nowhere
near the size of China’s.
This is due to the concentration of employment in low-value-added and largely informal sectors. The few manufacturing sectors with

high value-added and strong job potential — electrical equipment, electronics, automotive, chemicals and pharmaceuticals — continue to
face constraints from an unfavourable business environment and a skills shortage that is holding back their move into premium market
segments.
Furthermore, the highest-paying service sector jobs, particularly in information technology, are now threatened by the rise of
artificial intelligence. Without concrete measures, the “demographic window” — which is expected to remain favourable for another 15 to

25 years — could close before India establishes a solid industrial base and a sufficiently large middle class.
INDIA: A MIDDLE CLASS TOO SMALL TO SUPPLANT CHINA’S ECONOMIC ROLE
Johanna Melka
issue 20
26 October 2026

### Page 2

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 20 / 26 October 2026

for a changing
worldINDIA: A MIDDLE CLASS TOO SMALL TO SUPPLANT CHINA’S ECONOMIC ROLE
INDIA IS STRUGGLING TO ESTABLISH A ROBUST MIDDLE
CLASS
With an annual growth rate of 7.3% over the past decade (excluding the
pandemic), India has one of the highest growth rates among the world’s
major economies. Yet, its middle-class consumer base remains surpri-
singly small. When measured in current U.S. dollars, total consumption

is very high in India (USD 2,241 billion in 2025), but this figure prima -
rily reflects the country’s overall population size. By comparison, this
consumption level falls 3.5 times short of China’s corresponding figure.
Furthermore, per capita consumption adjusted for purchasing power
parity (PPP) is 1.3 times lower than that of Indonesia, the Philippines
and Vietnam. When evaluated at constant prices, consumption per ca-
pita has increased only slightly over the past thirty years compared to

China and even Vietnam (Chart 1).
According to our estimates, based on the research conducted by Ló -
pez-Calva and Ortiz-Juarez (2014) 1, India’s middle-class consumer
segment accounts for just 1.6% of the total population (see Table 1),
or fewer than 24 million people. This is less than in Thailand and Viet-
nam (more than 30 million each) and far below China (approximately
490 million, according to the same definition).
The majority of households identified as middle class by standard In -

dian research institutions (the Centre for Monitoring Indian Economy
[CMIE] and the National Council of Applied Economic Research [NCAER])
would, in fact, be categorised as “vulnerable” according to World Bank
criteria: households that have officially emerged from poverty yet lack
the financial stability necessary for sustained economic security.
Highly concentrated consumption and limited
upward mobility
The structure of Indian household consumption reveals significant

disparities between urban and rural areas. In 2024, average month -
ly consumption per capita amounted to 6,996 rupees in urban areas
1 These authors define the middle class as individuals for whom the risk of falling back into poverty is limited (less than 4% over four years), corresponding to daily per capita income
ranging from 17 to 98 2021 PPP dollars (with vulnerability thresholds at 6.85 and 14 2017 PPP dollars, according to the World Bank). In the absence of reliable national income

data collection mechanisms in India, our study applies these thresholds to the World Bank’s consumer expenditure data, an approach aligned with the objective of assessing actual
purchasing capacity deployed in the market (although this tends to slightly underestimate the middle class in terms of income, as household savings remain unaccounted for in
expenditure measurements).
(approximately USD 84.5), compared with 4,122 rupees in rural areas
(less than USD 50). The wealthiest 5% of urban consumers spent an

average equivalent of USD 245 per month per person. Consequently,
discretionary spending power remained concentrated among the hi -
ghest income brackets.
The structure of consumption has changed over the past decade. The
proportion allocated to food purchases (34.8% of total spending on ave-
rage) declined by 4.3 percentage points between 2011–12 and 2023–
24, while allocations to transportation and communications increased
to 12.4% (+3.8 pp). Although the allocation to durable goods has in -

creased, it remains below 7% of total household spending. This increase
was driven more by a rise in the use of credit than by income growth.
Over the past ten years, consumer loans have been the main source of
household debt. Although still modest relative to GDP , household debt
rose by 12.2 pp to reach 47.8% of GDP by the end of 2025, according to
the Bank for International Settlements (BIS).

THE LABOUR MARKET SUFFERS FROM STRUCTURAL
WEAKNESSES
The limited size of the middle class and the low level of household
consumption in India can be attributed, in particular, to the enduring
structural weaknesses of the labour market. The labour market faces
two major problems: insufficient job creation and the fact that most of
the jobs created are low-skilled and low-paid.

Insufficient job creation
Despite the Indian economy’s robust growth, its employment rate re -
mains substantially lower than in comparable Asian economies: 52.7%
in 2025 according to the International Labour Organization (ILO), com-
pared with 65.6% in Indonesia and 71.3% in Vietnam. This disparity
is mainly due to low labour force participation among women (34%
compared with 53.4% and 68%) and young people (40.8% of those aged
20-24 compared with 66.7% and 69.4%).

Over the past ten years, employment growth has averaged just 2.4%
annually, whereas GDP growth was close to 6% per year.
The services sector (representing 33.2% of employment in 2025) ac -
counted for the bulk of job creation during this period (+3.2% per year),
far ahead of the manufacturing sector (+1.4%) and, above all, agricultu-
re (+0.1%), which nevertheless remains the country’s largest employer
(41.5% of the workforce). This is not enough to absorb the 10 to 12 mil-

lion new entrants to the labour market each year.
2 Employment elasticity remained relatively stable between 1990 and 2012.
A limited number of sectors have significant po-
tential for creating high-quality, well-paid jobs
To identify sectors with high potential for job creation, we calculate
employment elasticity both for aggregate economy (KLEMS data) and
for the formal manufacturing sector alone (Annual Survey of Industries,
2025).
Over the period 1981–2023, aggregate employment elasticity remained

low at a virtually unchanged level, close to 0.3. This stability, however,
masks significant sectoral divergences: elasticity remained stable in
the services sector throughout the period (0.5), but it declined shar -
ply in the manufacturing sector (from 0.4 during 1981–1990 to just
0.1 during 2012–2023 2): at the same growth rate, India now creates
far fewer industrial jobs per unit of growth than it did forty years ago
(Table 2). In the formal manufacturing sector alone, employment elas-

ticity reached 0.7 over the past ten years, driven by very robust job
creation. However, this positive trend has not been sufficient to offset
the decline in informal sector employment, which accounts for 73.4%
of total manufacturing employment but only 38% of the sector’s value
added. Labour productivity in the formal manufacturing sector is thus
estimated to be 5.4 times higher than in the informal sector.
In capital-intensive sectors, employment elasticity ranged from 0.5 to

0.6 (including electrical equipment, transportation, chemicals and me-
tals), whereas in labour-intensive sectors — specifically wood, textiles
and apparel, and manufacturing food — employment elasticities re -
mained particularly low, or in certain cases, negative.

world
In other words, growth in value added in these sectors with high labour 
absorption potential is primarily driven by productivity gains rather 
than job creation. Furthermore, even among companies operating in 
the formal sector, employment elasticity remains low at an average of 
0.4. Consequently, the traditional mechanism facilitating labour mobi-
lity from the agricultural sector to the labour-intensive manufacturing 
sector has been very limited over the past forty years.
The only manufacturing subsectors that combine strong growth, high 
employment elasticity, and high productivity — thereby offering real 
potential for creating high-quality jobs — are electronics and infor -
mation technology, electrical equipment, the automotive industry, the 
chemical industry, and, to a lesser extent, the pharmaceutical industry. 
In these subsectors, the formal employment elasticity ranges from 0.8 
to 1.2 (Chart 2). However, in 2025, these jobs accounted for just 1.4% 
of total employment and 5.9% of GDP . The potential therefore remains 
negligible at the macroeconomic level.
Several structural constraints limit job creation in 
India
- The high rigidity of the labour market: it consists mainly of small, in-
formal businesses — those with fewer than 50 employees account for 
65% of the productive sector — largely due to the stringency of labour 
legislation (for example, companies with more than 300 employees 
cannot carry out mass layoffs without authorisation).
- Numerous obstacles impede the growth of industrial and job-crea -
ting businesses: an unfavourable business environment (particularly 
difficulties in accessing land acquisition to build factories); high costs 
of accessing capital (particularly for small and medium-sized enter -
prises); insufficient export competitiveness in labour-intensive sectors 
(textiles, clothing), which encounter disadvantages, including less ad -
vantageous tariff conditions3 than competing Asian economies.
- The mismatch between the skills of the available workforce and the 
needs of businesses. This mismatch constrains wage growth and the 
development of household purchasing power, obstructs industrial ad -
vancement, and, consequently, the creation of high-quality jobs.
INADEQUATE JOB QUALITY DUE TO A LACK OF  
APPROPRIATE SKILLS
A predominantly informal and low-paid labour  
market
According to the ILO, 87.2% of total employment in India is informal, 
with 73.4% concentrated in the manufacturing sector alone, predomi -
nantly engaged in low-value-added activities that require few skills. 
Furthermore, the Periodic Labour Force Survey (PLFS) 2023/24 indi -
cates that 58.4% of Indian workers are self-employed, 19.8% are day 
labourers, and only 21.7% are regular employees — the highest-paying 
status, with a wage gap of more than 60% compared to the other two 
categories. According to the ILO, 20% of the population captures 79% of 
aggregate labour income. The agricultural sector, which still employs 
41.5% of the labour force while contributing less than 15% to GDP , is

3 Historically, tariff rates imposed on Indian products exported to Europe have been higher than those applied to certain other Asian countries (Bangladesh, Vietnam, Pakistan).
4 According to the World Bank and the NCAER, educational and training programmes are too theoretical and fail to develop cognitive and interpersonal skills. Vocational education 
and training are not designed to meet the needs of high-demand sectors. Collaborative relationships between educational institutions and employers — particularly regarding 
internships and on-the-job training — remain inadequately developed.
3.7 times less productive than the services sector, with a median hourly 
wage that is 40 to 50% lower.
A widespread skills shortage
This concentration of earnings reflects the low skill levels of the jobs 
held: according to the PLFS, 88.2% of workers hold jobs requiring little 
or no skill. The proportion of skilled jobs is just 11.2% and has declined 
by 4.6 pp since 2018. The underlying challenge is not a shortage of 
graduates but rather a misalignment between what the labour market 
needs and the educational preparation provided to graduates 4, which 
results in their gradual downward mobility into positions far below 
their formal qualifications.
Unemployment, the downward mobility of graduates, and a shortage 
of technical skills
This disparity between skills and business needs directly contributes to 
unemployment among graduates, which reached 13.5% in 2025 accor-
ding to the ILO, compared with 1.3% for individuals without a degree. 
Within the 25- to 34-year-old demographic, the unemployment rate 
stands at 15.2% for graduates (19.2% for those with a master’s de -
gree), compared with an average of 6.1% across this age group. Only 
8.3% of graduates secure positions that genuinely correspond to their 
educational attainment; 53% of bachelor’s degree holders and 36% of 
master’s degree holders are overqualified for their positions.
In low-skilled jobs, the lack of technical training represents a signifi -
cant barrier to youth employment: according to the World Bank’s En -
terprise Survey (2022), 17.8% of traditional service companies and 14% 
of “intermediate” manufacturing companies cite this as a major obsta-
cle. In India’s manufacturing sector, 32.9% of positions are reportedly 
held by underqualified workers, according to the ILO, compared with 
28.8% in Indonesia and just 16.5% in Vietnam.
A direct impediment to industrial advancement
A survey conducted by the Ministry of Skill Development & Entrepre -
neurship across seven key sectors — automotive, textiles, agriculture, 
livestock, IT, commerce and green energy — reveals that the pace of

world
workforce training development significantly lags behind industry re -
quirements. This forces companies to train a considerable number of
young graduates themselves before they are ready for the workforce.
According to the Telecom Talent in the 5G Era report by the Telecom
Sector Skill Council, only 40% of IT and telecommunications graduates
are actually immediately employable.

This disparity in skills particularly constrains the transition towards
higher-value-added manufacturing activities within the sectors iden -
tified above (automotive, electronics and chemicals). Their growth
trajectory depends on technological advancement — electric vehicles,
semiconductors, specialty chemicals — and therefore on intermediate
to advanced technical skills that are currently lacking. Should trai -
ning provision fail to align with industry demand, employment elasti -

city and growth in these sectors will stagnate or decline, undermining
the only source of high-quality job creation currently available — and
consequently, the Indian economy’s ability to expand its middle class.
These structural limitations affecting national industrial development
diminish its appeal to foreign investors, despite its size. The stock of
foreign investment in India stood at USD 560 billion in Q2 2026, repre-
senting just 15% of GDP (compared with 20% in China, more than 52%

in Vietnam, and around 67% of GDP in Thailand and Malaysia).
ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE: A NEW RISK TO JOB CREATION
Artificial intelligence (AI) may also limit the expansion of high-paid
employment opportunities in the service sector. Its deployment poses
a significant threat to employment in India, particularly in the hi -
ghest-paying service sectors — the same sectors that possessed si -
gnificant potential for expanding India’s middle-class consumer base.

Agriculture (41.5% of the labour force), construction (12%), and trade/
transportation (18%) remain largely immune to the direct replacement
of workers by AI, which reduces the overall macroeconomic scale of
the coming shock. The service sectors offering the most lucrative em -
ployment — IT, finance and insurance — account for just 1.3% and 1.2%
of total employment, yet these remain the primary targets of automa-
tion. In IT services companies, the adoption of AI is expected to reduce

the need for entry-level roles (quality assurance, software testing and
support roles) but increase demand for positions centred on platform
development and management oversight. Current sector-specific evi -
dence indicates a notable slowdown in job creation within IT services
but no widespread contraction. A study by the Indian Council for Re -
search on International Economic Relations (ICRIER) on the IT sector
confirms a decline in job creation for routine and entry-level roles.

While new professions are emerging such as data centre operators, AI
DevOps engineers who serve as intermediaries between software deve-
lopment and systems operations teams, and ethical AI specialists, the
volume of employment opportunities generated remains substantially
lower than the number of jobs at risk. Furthermore, the skill require -
ments for these newly created positions exceed those possessed by the
existing cohort of graduates.
REFORMS ARE A STEP IN THE RIGHT DIRECTION BUT DO

NOT GO FAR ENOUGH
Since 2019, the Modi government has implemented a series of mea -
sures to develop industry, attract foreign direct investment, and gain
greater integration in global trade (after decades of protectionist po -
licies).
Corporate subsidy programmes (Production-Linked Incentives) have
helped support the development of those sectors with the greatest
potential for job creation (electrical, electronics, chemicals, and auto -
motive). Similarly, labour market reform (which came into force in late

2025) may alleviate impediments to business expansion and the crea-
tion of formal jobs. However, its actual impact will depend on its adop-
tion by the states, which retain considerable discretionary autonomy
over labour law, and on its effective implementation on the ground.
The free trade agreement signed between India and the European
Union in late January 2026 (which is expected to take effect in 2027 at
the earliest) should mitigate the tariff disadvantage experienced by la-

bour-intensive industries: the EU has pledged to remove tariffs, which
currently range from 4% to 12% on textiles and clothing.
However, these measures alone will be inadequate for creating
high-quality jobs if the education system fails to meet the needs of
Indian businesses. It is therefore imperative that India continues to
improve its business environment and, even more importantly, reform
its education system to promote job creation in high-value-added and

well-paying sectors. Without concrete measures, the “demographic
window” — which is expected to remain favourable for another 15 to
25 years — could close before India establishes a robust industrial
foundation and a substantial middle class.

While India’s economic growth has demonstrated considerable dy -
namism, this growth has not translated into the development of the
middle class. The real medium-term concern involves the emergence
of a “neo-precarious” middle class — substantial in terms of num -
bers but lacking the foundations necessary for advancing towards a
high-income economy (households with stable employment, the ability

to save, and social protection). The creation of formal, high-quality jobs
is currently inadequate to sustain industrial expansion and its progres-
sion towards higher-value segments. The cultivation of certain niche
activities, while promising, remains very limited and is far from benefi-
ting the entire population. Finally, the development of AI poses more of
a risk than a genuine opportunity for the country’s development. India
lacks both the infrastructure and the educational system necessary to

deploy AI in a way that would yield tangible benefits for its economy
and labour market.
Article completed on 19 October 2026

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
HOUSEHOLD SPENDING
INDICATOR BULLETIN
Spending resilient despite headwinds
4 November 2026
Key points
• Household spending rose 0.6%mth in
September, returning to growth after
August’s brief decline. Q3 spending increased
2.1%qtr, while volumes rose a more subdued

0.7%qtr, pointing to a sharp acceleration
in price growth through the quarter.
• Services spending rebounded and goods
spending remained supportive. Non-
discretionary spending outperformed
discretionary categories, with higher fuel
spending providing significant support.
• In real terms, spending growth over the
quarter was led by transport but with
most other categories also recording
gains. All states except Tasmania
recorded positive volume growth

in Q3, led by WA and Victoria.
• Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in
line with expectations and consistent with
our consumption outlook ahead of the Q3
National Accounts (due 2 December).

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel  which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.
